* 2026-10-01 `commands-exec`: vanilla orders a tick's `section_blocks_update`s across CHUNKS by identity hash — `ServerChunkCache.chunkHoldersToBroadcast` is a fastutil `ReferenceOpenHashSet` (constructor offset 44) — so a check whose one tick changes blocks in two chunks has a vanilla-side order no implementation can reproduce. Scenario 129's `fill` straddled a chunk edge and diverged on exactly that (within-section order matched); moved inside one chunk.
* 2026-10-01 `commands-exec`: `crates/rustmc-world/src/nbt.rs` wrote a compound's same-bucket keys in sorted order; vanilla's component codec inserts content before style, so `text` and `bold` (both bucket 11 of 16) came out reversed for any bold text component (scenario 134, `tellraw {text:"hello",color:gold,bold:true}`). Component compounds now insert in `ComponentSerialization`/`Style.Serializer` field order.
* 2026-10-01 `commands-exec` (landing repair): `crate::shortset::ShortOpenHashSet::insert` grew the table on `++size >= maxFill`; fastutil 8.5.18's `add` is `if (size++ >= maxFill)` (bytecode offsets 84-98: `dup_x1` keeps the old size for the compare), so vanilla's 32-slot set takes its 25th key before it rehashes, ours took 24. Every tick touching more than 24 blocks of one section came out in a different `section_blocks_update` order: scenario 129's 26-block hollow box swapped (2,5,6) ahead of (2,3,5),(1,3,4) (`~/.rustmc-detached/cmdexec2-rec2.log:29-60`). A simulation of both rules over that box at a section-y offset of 4 reproduces vanilla's order with fastutil's rule and ours with the old one, exactly; regression test `shortset::it_grows_after_the_key_that_reaches_max_fill`.
* 2026-10-01 `random-ticks`: our side read `absent` on every random-tick check because `brightness::Light` searched the whole radius-15 diamond per query (cost of a grass block's random tick in the open); the early-stop fix b5943f02c is verified on the wire. `ci/t2-gameplay-subset.sh` on 780+969..979, seed 20260803, Threadripper: rt-sub2 (tree before the fix, 04:18) declared 2/12 match, 8 absent; rt-sub5 (6be72b5e8, 15:32) declared 8/12 match, 0 absent — 780, 972, 973, 979 and the calibration all match (`~/.rustmc-detached/rt-sub{2,5}.log`, recordings `~/rustmc-rt2-keep/rt-sub5`).
* 2026-10-01 `random-ticks`: the third bone meal on a sweet berry bush did nothing on ours because `useitemon::PORTED_CLASSES` listed only `FlowerPotBlock`, so `SweetBerryBushBlock.useItemOn`'s PASS for bone meal (offsets 28-46) was missing: at age 2 the click fell to `useWithoutItem` (pick, age=1) and bone meal then wrote age=2 in the same tick. rt-sub5 975 diverged at observation 8, vanilla `level_event 1505` vs ours `block_update sweet_berry_bush[age=2]`. Fixed by listing the class (255d15639).
* 2026-10-01 `random-ticks`: the dig handler never sent `Block.spawnDestroyParticles`' 2001 (to everyone but the digger, 64 blocks); 970, 971, 974 read a random-ticked block's final state through it and diverged at observation 1 in rt-sub5 (vanilla 2001 data 5318/9/20944, ours block_update air). Ported; no other gameplay or survival check observes `level_event` with a dig in a measured step.
* 2026-10-01 `animals`: `ci/t2-gameplay-subset.sh:183` recorded vanilla without `--console`, so every `[[gamerule]]` a scenario declared was NOT APPLIED to the reference while the subject (its fifo always passed, `:264`) got it — a subset reading of any gamerule check compared two servers under different rules. Seen on `096-two-pigs-in-love-make-a-baby` (`mob_drops = false`), `~/rustmc-animals-rec/base.log` of job `anim-rec3`. Fixed in ad60722c2 (`--console "$VAN_DIR/console.fifo"`, as `ci/t2-gameplay.sh:912-913`).
* 2026-10-01 `animals`: `AgeableMob.makeAgeLockedParticle` (offsets 0-104) decrements a positive `ageLockParticleTimer` on BOTH sides — only the particle is client-side — and `canUseGoldenDandelion` waits for it to reach 0; `crate::mob::living` said "the timer stays 0 on the server". Ported in b0bd6f878; graded by `095`.
* 2026-10-01 `animals`: the server had no `ServerboundInteractPacket` (26) handler and the harness no verb to send one, so every animal mechanic behind `handleInteract` (feeding, love, breeding, spawn egg on a mob, golden dandelion, leads, taming) was untestable, not untested. Verb 979fe61c1, handler b0bd6f878.
* 2026-10-01 `animals`: the server has no block reach check on dig or use-item-on — `handle_player_action` (`crates/rustmc-server/src/world_send.rs:931`) and `handle_use_item_on` (`:2132`) never test vanilla's `isWithinBlockInteractionRange(pos, 1.0)`, (4.5 + 1.0)^2 = 30.25 from the eye. `101`'s first rig stood at x=0 and dug and spawn-egged a pit at x=5 (eye-to-box 31.9 and 38.1): vanilla refused both, placed no second pig and its `newest` interact hit the pig already in love; ours placed it and fed it (`~/rustmc-animals-rec2/run1/{vanilla,rustmc}.json`, scenario `two-pigs-in-love-four-blocks-apart-never-breed`, step 1: vanilla 0 `entity_event`, ours 1). Not ported here (it would move every reach-dependent check on the box); `101` now stands between its pits.
* 2026-10-01 `passive-mobs`: this server had no level seed — `status::WORLD_SEED` is `0`, the corpus's vanilla is booted at `GAMEPLAY_SEED` 20260803 (`ci/t2-gameplay.sh:137`) — so every loot table naming a `random_sequence` (`RandomSequences.get(id, getSeed())`: shearing, chicken lay, villager trades) could only match vanilla by accident. `--level-seed`, passed by both gameplay runners; `/seed` and the login hash still read `WORLD_SEED`.
* 2026-10-01 `passive-mobs`: a sheep's wool count is drawn from the OUTER table's stream, `minecraft:shearing/sheep` — the per-colour `shearing/sheep/<colour>` tables carry their own `random_sequence` but run nested inside the outer `LootContext` (`Sheep.shear` 14 passes `SHEAR_SHEEP`), so their ids are never asked for.
* 2026-10-01 `passive-mobs`: the shared `Animal.mobInteract` path called `pig::eat_sound` for any animal, which reads accessor 20 (`PIG_SOUND_VARIANT`) and panics on a kind without it; `Animal.playEatingSound` is empty (javap 0: `return`) and only `Pig` overrides it. Latent while the pig was the only animal.
* 2026-10-01 `xp-orbs`: main never sent `set_experience` at all — `crates/rustmc-server/src/respawn.rs:228` `encode_set_experience` had no caller — while vanilla sends it on every player's first `doTick` (`ServerPlayer.<init>` offset 60 `lastSentExp = -99999999`, `doTick` offsets 488-529 compare `totalExperience != lastSentExp`), so every join on main was one packet short; now sent from `crate::experience::connection_tick`.
* 2026-10-01 `xp-orbs`: an orb from `ExperienceOrb.award` draws a yaw and three velocity doubles from its own unseeded random (`ExperienceOrb.<init>(Level,Vec3,Vec3,int)` offsets 20-102), so its `add_entity` y_rot/movement are vanilla-against-vanilla noise; a `/summon`ed orb goes through `ExperienceOrb(EntityType,Level)` and draws only the uuid, which is why 682-684 grade summoned orbs and 685 (furnace, `award`) grades only the pickup.
* 2026-10-01 `xp-orbs`: `tryMergeToExisting` draws `ServerLevel.getRandom().nextInt(40)` per award piece and merges only into an orb with `(id - r) % 40 == 0` (`ExperienceOrb.tryMergeToExisting` offsets 8-19, `canMerge(ExperienceOrb,int,int)` 7-16): a second award beside a live orb of the same value merges with probability ~1/40 per candidate, unseeded — not gradeable, ported with the level random.
* 2026-10-01 `xp-orbs`: `crates/rustmc-server/src/tracker.rs` sent dirty entity data every tick BEFORE the `sendChanges` gate, citing "offset 204"; in 26.2 offset 205 is inside the item-frame map branch and an ordinary entity reaches `sendDirtyEntityData` at offset 1093, after the velocity (1061) and position (1076) packets, with `entityData.isDirty()` forcing the body off the interval (offsets 250-281). Vanilla's 684 recording shows `move_entity_heartbeat` then the summoned orb's second `set_entity_data`. Fixed in `ServerEntity::send_changes_with`; mob scenarios 080-089 re-recorded to check.
* 2026-10-01 `xp-orbs`: on main no furnace fed through its menu ever finishes a smelt: `crate::cooking::set_item` (the port of `AbstractFurnaceBlockEntity.setItem`'s cook-time reset) has no caller, so `cooking_total_time` stays 0 and `cooking.rs:192`'s `cooking_timer == cooking_total_time` never holds. Vanilla's recording of raw gold after 11 s shows the ingot taken to the cursor; ours shows only the fuel slot's correction. `origin/be-furnace-brewing` adds `set_item_from_menu`; the experience payout check is parked at `docs/cold-start/xp-orbs-685-for-be-furnace-brewing.toml` until it lands.
* 2026-10-01 `train-f`: `animals` and `xp-orbs` each made an experience orb: `animals`' `mob::breed::add_experience_orb` inserted an entity no tick ever reached, `xp-orbs`' `crate::xporb` registers and ticks its own; on one tree the breeding orb would have hung where it was made. Breeding now spawns through `xporb::spawn_with_direction` (`crates/rustmc-server/src/mob/breed.rs`, `finalize_spawn_child_from_breeding`), and a mob's drops and a dropped lead register in `itementity::ItemEntities` (`mob/drops.rs` `register_item`, `mob/leash.rs` `spawn_lead_at`).
* 2026-10-01 `train-f`: in `683`'s window vanilla tells the actor the observer's `entity_position` (`[0, 0, 5]` relative, before the summon; `scratchpad/xp/runs/r3/vanilla.json`, `an-orb-four-blocks-away-creeps-to-the-player`) and this server sends nothing for that player; at the default `within = 16` that made an orb check diverge on a player's sync. Bounded to `within = 4`; `oracle-runner compare` on the same pair: declared and strict `match`.
* 2026-10-01 `train-f`: on an item pickup vanilla sends `take_item_entity`, then the hotbar `container_set_slot`, then `remove_entities`; this server sends the `remove_entities` before the slot (`~/pickup-rec/fixed-2/{vanilla,rustmc}.json`, `a-thrown-item-is-picked-up-when-its-delay-runs-out` and `two-thrown-stones-merge-and-are-picked-up-as-one`). The two checks are baselined `diverge` on that order alone.
* 2026-10-01 `train-f`: six lane checks cannot be graded as written, by recording: `096` errors on vanilla (pigs never reach the client, `~/rustmc-animals-rec3/branch.log:62`), `641`/`646`/`654` are vacuous (a silent second click whose setup carries all the traffic, `~/rustmc-passive/oracle-work/gameplay/pm-subset-2.log`), `994`/`998` are inert (`~/pickup-rec/fixed-2/subset.log`). Parked in `docs/cold-start/train-f-parked/`.
* 2026-10-01 `train-g`: `entity-ports` and the loot lane graded the same no-entity loot roll opposite ways — entity-ports' `entity_dispatch.rs` asserted an entity table with an empty `EntityCtx` must refuse, the loot lane's `golden_loot.rs` (`entity_tables_with_no_entity_reproduce_vanillas_vectors`) reproduces the jar's own vectors for that roll, which answer `false` without a draw. Kept vanilla's: `crates/rustmc-loot/src/lib.rs` `entity_slot` has no `NoEntityContext` arm, and the entity-ports test is replaced by `an_entity_table_without_a_context_answers_as_an_unattended_death`.
* 2026-10-01 `train-g`: on `entity-ports`, a mob's death loot could never roll on a real server: `mob::drops::death_loot` refused "the random-sequence store has no world seed" unless `randomsequences::init` had run, and nothing outside `tests/mob_combat.rs` called it (`main.rs` set only `lootroll`'s seed, `--seed`). Folded into `crate::lootroll::sequences()`. A third store survives: `train-f`'s `mob::drops::random_sequence` (shearing, chicken laying), seeded from `--level-seed` (`status::level_seed`) — the harness passes the same value to both flags (`ci/t2-gameplay.sh`, its `--level-seed` and `--seed` lines).
* 2026-10-01 `train-g`: three branches each gave `ItemStack` its own data-component store — item-components' `patch: Option<Box<Patch>>` (names, raw creative values, `HolderIds`), menus-workstations' `components: Components` (typed, enchantments as numeric ids from `enchantment::Registry`), mining's `damage: i32` + `enchantments: Vec` fields with a third `Component::Enchantments(Vec<(i32, i32)>)` writer in `dig.rs`. One store now (`crates/rustmc-server/src/item.rs`, the patch); `Patch` equality is `Map.equals` (`patch.rs`), which the derive was not — two stacks with the same two enchantments in different orders were not `is_same_item_same_components`.
* 2026-10-01 `train-g`: three branches each ported a player's experience — entity-ports in `playertick::LivePlayer` (its own `lastSentExp` in `playersync`), commands-exec on the connection (play-loop sender), train-f in `experience::Registry` (clock-thread sender). Merged as-is the join would have sent `set_experience` two or three times. One store and one sender now: `experience::connection_tick`.
* 2026-10-01 `train-g`: `entity-ports`' death orbs (`xporb::award` -> `drops::orb_entity`) were entities no tick reached and no pickup could take — inserted and tracked, never registered with an orb store; `train-f`'s `crate::xporb` registers, ticks and picks up its own. A death now awards through train-f's (`mob/mod.rs` `spawn_experience`).
* 2026-10-01 `train-g`: `ItemEntity.DATA_ITEM` had three encodings across the train — be-hopper-dispense's `Synched::Item { value }` (full `OPTIONAL_STREAM_CODEC` bytes), mining's `Synched::Item { item, count }`, train-f's `Synched::Stack { item_id, count }` — and four dirty-data writers reached the tracker by four routes. `Synched::item` builds the patchless one; every writer is a `tracker::DirtySource` (`tracker.rs` `Drained`, `dispenser::ItemSync`, `synched::DroppedSync`).
* 2026-10-01 `train-g`: the dug block's own `2001` was sent twice on one tree — `random-ticks`' `fac5ee3ee` added `spawn_destroy_particles` to the old dig handler, and `mining`'s `dig::destroy_block` already sends `Block.spawnDestroyParticles` to everyone but the digger. Mining's kept.

* 2026-10-02 **Every structure block figure for end cities and mansions read 0 while 0 of their 63 block entities existed.** `region-differ bediff` vanilla first, in-box (the start's saved `Children`), dumps with `RUSTMC_RECORD_BLOCK_ENTITIES=1`: end city seed 20260722 (83,62) 0/20, mansion seed 8 (32,16) 0/7, seed 30 (37,-47) 0/36; floors 20/20, 7/7, 36/36. Now 20/20, 7/7, 36/36 with LootTableSeeds (`crates/rustmc-server/tests/structure_block_entities.rs`). A block-state diff cannot see a chest's table or a banner's patterns; any structure lane should run `bediff` beside `diff`.
* 2026-10-02 Both the mansion and end city placers passed `None` as `place_piece`'s stream, so `placeInWorld`'s `nextLong` per tagged template container was never drawn and every later seed on that chunk moved: the mansion's (590,88,-747) read -237254229101828014 against vanilla's 8157007271250325635. The mansion also drew its marker chests' seeds and dropped them; the end city never ran its `Chest` marker.
* 2026-10-02 The emitter withheld every chest's template `Items` and refused a metadata-only payload, so a mansion's 26-chest storeroom (`Items:[]`), its chests of alliums and saplings, every plain banner and the end city's ender chest were absent. Census window (seed 12345 r16, `ci/be-payload-r16-12345.sh`) unchanged by the fix: 99/99 exact, missing 74, spurious 3.
* 2026-10-02 `rsync -a` into a dev dir copies the worktree's mtimes, so restoring a file after a by-hand sabotage leaves it OLDER than the build artefacts and cargo reuses the sabotaged binary: the restored tree read red. Sync with `--checksum` and without `-t`, or `touch` after.
* 2026-10-02 **The end city ship branch is verified.** `worldgen-dump end-city-starts 20260722 0 0 200` finds 37 cities, 21 with ships; clamped twin End harvests of two (`sl2-ec1` start (81,3), 20 pieces; `sl2-ec2` start (-176,-99), 15 pieces) match our piece boxes 20/20 and 15/15 in order, blocks 0 and 85 of 5,308,416 against floors 0 and 85 (all 85 chorus outside the boxes), block entities 6/6 and 12/12 after adding `brewing_stand` (template `Items`/`BrewTime`/`Fuel`) and `skull` (metadata only) to the emitter.

Seed 30 (37,-47) read 96: 42 were a mirrored door's hinge and a mirrored chest pair (fixed, 54 left, same vegetation classes).
* 2026-10-02 **`TemplateStructurePiece.postProcess`'s data markers are clipped to the chunk.** `filterBlocks` drops every position outside `placeSettings.getBoundingBox()`, which `postProcess` sets to the chunk box first; a marker's draw happens only in its own chunk's pass. Measured: seed 12345's cold ocean ruin at (65,67) spans four chunks and vanilla's chest seed is draw 2 of (64,66), the chest's chunk; drawing in all four gave draw 2 of (65,67). Any port that handles markers per chunk must test the marker's own position against the chunk.
* 2026-10-02 `Rule::parse` dropped `block_entity_modifier` without refusing it, so every trail ruin's 86-88 suspicious blocks had no `LootTable`/`LootTableSeed` and the rule's positional stream skipped a draw. Trail ruins in-box 0/91 -> 88/91 and 0/90 -> 86/90 (floors exact).

* 2026-10-02 `nether-parity`: a by-hand sabotage restored with `cp` + `touch` in a remote dir is undone by the next `rsync -a` from the worktree, which sets the file's mtime back to the worktree's — older than the sabotaged artifact — so cargo reuses the sabotage. Measured: a full nether window run on `~/rustmc-nether-parity-b` reported 6,694 mushroom refusals the committed code does not make. After any sabotage, `touch` every source after the LAST rsync, not before it.

* 2026-10-04 The gate chain rebased branches that lane worktrees had checked out: out-of-tree `~/grit/gate-chain.sh` ran `git checkout -B <br> origin/<br>` then `rebase` in its own worktree, which rewrites the shared `refs/heads/<br>`, and git 2.43's `checkout -B` does not refuse a branch checked out elsewhere. The holding worktree then stages the reverse of every main commit the rebase picked up. Seen at `~/grit/gate-chain.log:8016132-8016135` (bench-refresh, 3 Oct 21:53) and `:8016284-8016287` (mobs-undead, 46 commits, 22:21); the log is archived under `~/grit/logs-archive/gate-chain.log.20261004-*.gz` with the same line numbers. Reproduced end to end by `~/grit/chain-hardening-test.sh` against the pre-fix script: 16 of 27 cases fail, including "main LOST m2 — a revert landed".
* 2026-10-04 A worktree DETACHED at a branch's old tip is not affected when that branch's ref moves (its HEAD is a sha): `~/grit/chain-hardening-test.sh` section 3 shows its status and its next commit carry only its own edits. Only a worktree holding the branch symbolically is at risk; the chain now skips it when dirty or when a process lives in it, and detaches it first when clean and idle.
* 2026-10-04 `systemctl --user stop grit-supervisor.service` does not stop the gate chain: the unit is `KillMode=process` (`~/.config/systemd/user/grit-supervisor.service`), so the keeper and the chain it launched keep running. `~/grit/halt-chain.sh` is the stop. The previous wording in `~/grit/RESUME-NOW.md:14` said otherwise.
* 2026-10-04 Killing a gate chain's driver does not free the gate: the in-flight `ci/handoff.sh` inherits the chain's flock fd 200, so a relaunched chain logs `another chain holds the gate; waiting` (`~/grit/gate-chain.log`, 11:00:30) and starts only when that gate ends. Safe, but a restart is not instant.
* 2026-10-04 The supervisor's 24h no-landing alert (`~/grit/supervisor.sh` `check_no_landing`) counted `handoff rc=0  MERGED` lines, so any log rotation reset its clock; with daily rotation it could never fire. It now keys on the last MERGED line; `~/grit/supervisor-falsify.sh` case 10 fails on the old body and passes on the new.
* 2026-10-04 /home free space fell from 217 GB (10:29) to 169 GB (10:56) while lanes built, `df -BG /home`; `du -s --block-size=1G` put 50 GB in `~/rustmc-stack/target` and 1-28 GB in each of 23 other `~/rustmc-*/target` (all touched 3-4 Oct, so none is reapable by the 3-day rule). The chain and keeper now stop gating below 40 GB free.
* 2026-10-04 A clean lane worktree can still be in use: on 4 Oct `~/grit/work-benchrefresh` and `~/grit/work-undead` held their branches symbolically with nothing uncommitted while a detached runner (`~/.rustmc-detached/benchrefresh-chain4.body.sh`) had its cwd there. Detaching such a worktree, as `ci/handoff.sh` `release_worktree` does for any clean holder, would put the runner's next commit on a detached HEAD and make its `git push origin <br>` push the moved ref instead; the chain now treats a live process inside a holder as held (`~/grit/chain-hardening-test.sh` section 12).
